e:T71aa2,<h2 id="기본-사용법">기본 사용법<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#기본-사용법"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<p style="font-size: 25px; font-weight: bold;">react testing library</p>
<ul>
<li>
<p>설치</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">npm</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> install</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> --save-dev</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> @testing-library/react</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">or</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">yarn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> -D</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> @testing-library/react</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>사용할때 주의할점</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p>참고 url</p>
<p><a href="https://seongry.github.io/2021/06-20-common-mistakes-with-rty/">https://seongry.github.io/2021/06-20-common-mistakes-with-rty/</a>
<a href="https://kentcdodds.com/blog/common-mistakes-with-react-testing-library">Common mistakes with React Testing Library</a></p>
<ul>
<li>
<p>destructure what you need from render or call it view.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ❌</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> wrapper</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Example</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> prop</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"1"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">wrapper.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">rerender</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Example</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> prop</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"2"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ✅</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">rerender</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Example</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> prop</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"1"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">rerender</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Example</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> prop</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"2"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>automatically Cleanup</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ❌</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { render, screen, cleanup } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@testing-library/react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">afterEach</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(cleanup);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ✅</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { render, screen } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@testing-library/react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>대부분의 메이저 프레임워크(mocha, Jest, and Jasmine)에서 자동적으로 cleanup 함수가 호출됨으로 각 테스트 마다 cleanup 함수를 호출해 줄 필요가 없다.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>screen을 사용할 것</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ❌ 사용해야 할 기능이 많아 질 수 있다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Example</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> errorMessageNode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'alert'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ✅ 사용해야 할 기능이 많아져도 추가되는 코드가 없다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Example</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> errorMessageNode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'alert'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// debug 함수 대신에 screen.debug 호출해주세요..</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">debug</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> 엘리먼트 구조를 보여줌</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>react testing library에서는 메소드를 불러오는 방식으로 두가지 방식으로 지원한다</p>
<ol>
<li>메소드를 import 해오는 방법</li>
<li>screen 객체에서 참조해 쓰는방법
screen 사용하게 되면 엘리먼트 추가/삭제/업데이트가 될 경우 최신상태를 얻기 위해서 비구조화 할당을 하지 않고 엘리먼트의 속성들을 접근이 가능함.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>
<p>expect의 검증 메소드를 정확하게 사용하도록 할 것</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> screen.screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByTestId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'button'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ❌</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(button.disabled).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// error message:</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//  expect(received).toBe(expected) // Object.is equality</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//  Expected: true</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//  Received: false</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ✅</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(button).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeDisabled</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// error message:</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//   Received element is not disabled:</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//     &#x3C;button /></span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>다음 코드와 같이 expect 뒤에 들어갈 메소드가 좀더 구체적일 경우 에러메세지를 디버깅이 수월하게 띄워준다. 원활한 디버깅을 위해 정확한 검증 메소드를 사용하자</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>getByText 사용에 지양</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> data-testid</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"helloWorld"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Hello World&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 1. Wrong! 😓</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">/hello world/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 2. Best! 😄</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByTestId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'helloWorld'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>fireEvent 사용에 주의할 것</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ❌</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">fireEvent.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">change</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(input, { target: { value: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'hello world'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ✅</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">userEvent.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(input, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'hello world'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>fireEvent는 유저 행동에 친숙한 방식의 메서드로 설정되어 있어서 input 이벤트가 변경될 때(keyDown, keyPress, keyUp) 호출되게 되다 보니, 개발자 측면에서는 혼란을 줄 수가 있습니다.
fireEvent 보다는 useEvent 메서드는 이벤트의 특성에 따라 호출됨으로 원할한 디버깅을 위해서는 userEvent를 사용해야 합니다.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>find에 waitFor이 내장되어 있다</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ❌</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> submitButton</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> waitFor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  screen.screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByTestId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'submit'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ✅</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> submitButton</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">findByTestId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'submit'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>find로 조합되는 메소드들은 모두 waitFor이 내장 되어있다. find 혹은 waitFor을 쓰게 된다면 이점 주의해서 사용하도록 하자.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Wrapping things in act unnecessarily
render, fireEvent에서 자체적으로 act를 래핑하고 있기 때문에 불필요하게 사용할 필요가 없습니다.
act에 사용용도는 state 상태를 변경 시 수동으로 DOM을 업데이트 할 경우 호출되는 메서드입니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ❌</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">act</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Example</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">})</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> input</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'textbox'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, {name:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> /choose a fruit/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">})</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">act</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  fireEvent.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">keyDown</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(input, {key: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'ArrowDown'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">})</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">})</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ✅</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Example</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> input</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'textbox'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, {name:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> /choose a fruit/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">})</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">fireEvent.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">keyDown</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(input, {key: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'ArrowDown'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">})</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>잘못된 쿼리 사용 개선
사용자 동선과 가깝게 쿼리 진행하기</p>
<pre><code>// ❌
// assuming you've got this DOM to work with:
// &#x3C;label>Username&#x3C;/label>&#x3C;input data-testid="username" />
screen.getByTestId('username')

// ✅
// change the DOM to be accessible by associating the label and setting the type
// &#x3C;label for="username">Username&#x3C;/label>&#x3C;input id="username" type="text" />
screen.getByRole('textbox', {name: /username/i})
</code></pre>
<p>container/querySelector 사용한 요소 쿼리 지양</p>
<pre><code>// ❌
const { container } = render(&#x3C;Example />);
const button = container.querySelector(".btn-primary");
expect(button).toHaveTextContent(/click me/i);
// ✅
render(&#x3C;Example />);
screen.getByRole("button", { name: /click me/i });
</code></pre>
<p>→ 용도에 맞는 query 함수 사용</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>불필요한 접근성 속성은 추가하지 않기</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="jsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="jsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ❌</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> role</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"button"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Click me&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ✅</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// button element에서는 기본적으로 role 속성이 button임으로 role=button 추가하지 않기</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Click me&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>존재하지 않는 엘리먼트를 체크하는 외에는 query* 문을 사용하지 않기</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="jsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="jsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ❌</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">queryByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'alert'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeInTheDocument</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ✅</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'alert'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeInTheDocument</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">queryByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'alert'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).not.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeInTheDocument</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>Query</p>
<ul>
<li>
<p>query vs get vs find
<strong>query</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>queryBy일치하는 1개의 엘리먼트를 검색한다. 먼저 querySelectorAll로 모든 엘리먼트를 찾고 없을 경우 null을, 1개일 경우 노드 리스트의 첫 번째 엘리먼트를 반환한다. 2개 이상일 경우에는 에러를 던진다.</li>
<li>queryAllBy일치하는 모든 엘리먼트를 검색한다. querySelectorAll의 결과를 그대로 반환한다.
<strong>get</strong></li>
<li>getBy일치하는 1개의 엘리먼트를 검색한다. 먼저 querySelectorAll로 모든 엘리먼트를 찾고 1개일 경우 노드 리스트의 첫 번째 엘리먼트를 반환한다. 없거나 2개 이상일 경우에는 에러를 던진다.</li>
<li>getAllBy일치하는 모든 엘리먼트를 검색한다. querySelectorAll의 결과를 그대로 반환한다. 일치하는 엘리먼트가 없을 경우 에러를 던진다.
<strong>find</strong>
Promise를 리턴하는 것만 제외하면 get 타입과 동일하다. <a href="https://testing-library.com/docs/dom-testing-library/api-async/#waitfor">waitFor</a> 메서드를 감싸는 형태로 되어 있어서 에러를 던지지 않을 때까지 검색을 재시도한다.
<strong>어느 경우에 사용을 해야할까??</strong>
query는 엘리먼트가 없어도 에러를 던지지 않기 때문에 엘리먼트의 존재 여부를 확인하는 데 적합하다. get 을 기본 옵션으로 사용하고 엘리먼트가 렌더링될 때까지 기다려야 하는 상황이 있다면 find를 사용하는 게 좋지 않을까 싶다.
<img src="/blog-assets/2022/07/images/24/Untitled.png" alt="Untitled"></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p>쿼리 우선순위</p>
<ul>
<li>시각 요소/ 사용자의 인터렉션을 테스트하기 위한 쿼리들 간 우선순위
<ul>
<li>getByRole > getByLabelText > getByPlaceholderText > getByText > getByDisplayValue</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>HTML5 와 ARIA 속성을 테스트 하기 위한 쿼리들 간 우선순위
<ul>
<li>getByAltText > getByTitle</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>테스트 ID: 위에 쿼리들로도 불가능한 값을 검색하기 위해서만 사용
<ul>
<li>getByTestId
<a href="https://testing-library.com/docs/queries/about/#priority">https://testing-library.com/docs/queries/about/#priority</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p>ByRole</p>
<ul>
<li>API: getByRole, queryByRole, getAllByRole, queryAllByRole, findByRole, findAllByRole</li>
<li>설명
<ul>
<li>역할을 가진 요소를 dom에서 얻어올 경우 사용</li>
<li>웹접근성에 aria-role 의미와 일치하는 키값을 통해서 dom을 얻어 올 수 있음.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>참고 URL
<ul>
<li><a href="https://testing-library.com/docs/queries/byrole">https://testing-library.com/docs/queries/byrole</a></li>
<li>자주 사용되는 role 정리: <a href="https://www.w3.org/TR/html-aria/#sotd">https://www.w3.org/TR/html-aria/#sotd</a>
<a href="https://www.notion.so/480e494982234b839e945e225b4642dc">제목 없음</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>

<p style="font-size: 25px; font-weight: bold;">Jest Expect</p>
<ul>
<li>
<p>.toBe(value)
toBe를 사용하여 기본값을 비교하거나 Object 인스턴스의 참조 ID(<strong>Shallow compare</strong>)를 체크할 수 있습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> can</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'pamplemousse'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ounces: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">12</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">describe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'the can'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'has 12 ounces'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(can.ounces).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">12</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'has a sophisticated name'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(can.name).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'pamplemousse'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'toBe는 obj가 같은 객체를 가리키고 있는지 확인한다'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> obj</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {};</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(obj).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(obj); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// true</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'객체의 내용이 같더라도 서로 다른 메모리에 있는 객체이기 때문에 toBe를 쓰면 false가 나온다.'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'John'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'John'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// false</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>.toEqual(value)
객체 인스턴스의 모든 속성을 재귀적으로 비교할 수 있습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> can1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  flavor: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'grapefruit'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ounces: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">12</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> can2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  flavor: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'grapefruit'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ounces: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">12</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">describe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'the La Croix cans on my desk'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'have all the same properties'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(can1).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toEqual</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(can2);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'are not the exact same can'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(can1).not.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(can2);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'대신에 객체의 내용이 같은지를 확인하려면 toEqual을 써야 한다'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> obj</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {};</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'John'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toEqual</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'John'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// true</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>.toBeNull(), toBeUndefined()
.toBeNull()은 .toBe(null)과 동일하지만 오류 메시지가 조금 더 좋습니다. 따라서 무언가가 null인지 확인하려면 .toBeNull()을 사용하십시오
.toBeUndefined()은 .toBeUndefined(null)과 동일하지만 오류 메시지가 조금 더 좋습니다. 따라서 무언가가 undefined인지 확인하려면 .toBeUndefined()을 사용하십시오</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> bloop</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'bloop returns null'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">bloop</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">()).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeNull</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'the best drink for octopus flavor is undefined'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">bestDrinkForFlavor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'octopus'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeUndefined</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>.toBeTruthy(), .toBeFalsy()
.toBeTruthy()는 .toBeFalsy의 반대로, 값이 무엇인지와는 상관없이 boolean 컨텍스트에서 값이 true인지 여부를 확인할 때 사용하게 됩니다.
느슨한 타입 기반 언어인 자바스크립트는 true/false 가 boolean 타입만으로 간주되지 않습니다. 숫자 1이 true, 숫자 0이 false로 간주되게 되는데 그 느슨한 타입 기반을 체크하는 할 경우 toBeTruthy, toBeFalsy를 사용하면 됩니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'number 0 is falsy but string 0 is truthy'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeFalsy</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'0'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeTruthy</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>.toHaveLength(number)
.toHaveLength는 객체에 .length 속성이 있고 특정 숫자값으로 설정되어 있는지 여부를 확인할 수 있습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveLength</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'abc'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveLength</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).not.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveLength</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>.toHaveProperty(keyPath, value?)
.toHaveProperty는 객체안에 참조 keyPath의 속성이 존재하는지 테스트할 때 사용할 수 있습니다.
수신된 특정 값 비교를 위해서는, 선택적으로 value 인수를 제공할 수 있습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Object containing house features to be tested</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> houseForSale</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  bath: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  bedrooms: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  kitchen: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    amenities: [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'oven'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'stove'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'washer'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    area: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">20</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    wallColor: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'white'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>

</li>
<li>
<p>.toHaveBeenCalled()
.toHaveBeenCalled()는 모의 함수가 호출 되었는지 확인할 수 있습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> drinkAll</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">callback</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">flavour</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (flavour </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">!==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'octopus'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    callback</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(flavour);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">describe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'drinkAll'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'drinks something lemon-flavoured'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> drink</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> jest.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    drinkAll</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(drink, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'lemon'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(drink).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveBeenCalled</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// drink 모의함수가 호출여부 확인(callback)</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'does not drink something octopus-flavoured'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> drink</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> jest.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    drinkAll</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(drink, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'octopus'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(drink).not.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveBeenCalled</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// drink 모의함수가 호출여부 확인(callback)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>.arrayContaining(array)
.arrayContaining(array)는 수신된 배열에 예상되는 배열의 요소들이 포함되는지 판단합니다.
즉, 예상되는 배열을 수신된 배열의 하위 집합입니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">describe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'arrayContaining'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> expected</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Alice'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Bob'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  it</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'matches even if received contains additional elements'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Alice'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Bob'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Eve'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toEqual</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(expect.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">arrayContaining</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(expected));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  it</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'does not match if received does not contain expected elements'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Bob'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Eve'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]).not.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toEqual</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(expect.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">arrayContaining</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(expected));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>.toContain</p>
<ul>
<li>배열의 아이템을 포함되어 있는지 여부를 체크해야할 경우 사용</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'the flavor list contains lime'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'lime'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'mangle'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toContain</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'lime'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>수신된 문자열이 예상된 문자열이 포함되었는지 여부를 체크할 경우 사용</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'the flavor list contains lime'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'lime juice'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toContain</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'lime'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">mockFn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">30</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">40</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'10이랑 20을 인수로 전달받은 함수가 있는가'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(mockFn).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">lastCalledWith</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">30</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">40</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>

<li>
<p>.toMatchSnapshot
파일 스냅샷 테스트 지원을 해야할 경우 사용
테스트 완료 수 <strong>snapshots</strong> 디렉토리가 생성되고, 그 안에 repeat.test.js.snap 파일이 생성됨.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> repeat </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> './repeat'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'repeats words three times'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>

<li>
<p>.toBeRequired
엘리먼트의 속성에 required, aria-required=”true”가 존재하는지 여부를 체크하기 위해서 사용</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// example</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">input</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> data-testid</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"required-input"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> required</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">input</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> data-testid</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"aria-required-input"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> aria-required</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"true"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">input</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> data-testid</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"conflicted-input"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> required</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> aria-required</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"false"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>

<li>
<p>.toBeGreaterThan(number | bigint)
수신받은 데이터(received)보다 예상되는 데이터(expected) 보다 큰 숫자일 여부를 체크하기 위해서 사용</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'ounces per can is more than 10'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">ouncesPerCan</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">()).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeGreaterThan</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ouncesPerCan() 이 10 온스 이상의 값을 반환 하는지 테스트</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
</ul>
<p style="font-size: 25px; font-weight: bold;">User Event</p>
<p>package: @testing-library/user-event</p>
<ul>
<li>
<p>설치</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">npm</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> install</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> --save-dev</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> @testing-library/user-event</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">or</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">yarn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> -D</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> @testing-library/user-event</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>API</p>
<ul>
<li>
<p>click(element, eventInit, options)</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> React </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { render, screen } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@testing-library/react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> userEvent </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@testing-library/user-event'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'click'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">label</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> htmlFor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"checkbox"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Check&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">label</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">input</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"checkbox"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"checkbox"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  userEvent.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Check'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByLabelText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Check'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeChecked</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">userEvent.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(elem, { ctrlKey: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, shiftKey: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">userEvent.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(elem, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">undefined</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { skipPointerEventsCheck: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> });</span></span></code></pre></figure>
