<li>
<p>selectOptions(element, values, options) / deselectOptions(element, values, options)</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> React </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'selectOptions'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>

<li>
<p>tab({shift, focusTrap})</p>
<ul>
<li>브라우저와 동일하게 tab 이벤트를 발생시켜서 activeElement를 변경 시킨다.</li>

<li>
<p>hover(element, options) / unhover(element, options)</p>
<pre><code>```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import Tooltip from '../tooltip';

test('hover', () => {
  const messageText = 'Hello';
  render(
    &#x3C;Tooltip messageText={messageText}>
      &#x3C;TrashIcon aria-label="Delete" />
    &#x3C;/Tooltip>,
  );

  userEvent.hover(screen.getByLabelText(/delete/i));
  expect(screen.getByText(messageText)).toBeInTheDocument();
  userEvent.unhover(screen.getByLabelText(/delete/i));
  expect(screen.queryByText(messageText)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
});
```
</code></pre>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="api-유닛테스트">API 유닛테스트<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#api-유닛테스트"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<p style="font-size: 25px; font-weight: bold;">패키지</p>
<ul>
<li>jest</li>
<li>react-test-renderer</li>
<li>axios, fetch</li>
</ul>
<p style="font-size: 25px; font-weight: bold;">테스트 사항</p>
<ul>
<li>HTTP 성공여부 확인(request code 200)</li>
<li>API 기대응답에 대한 비교</li>
<li>API 응답이 길 경우 속성의 길이만 테스트</li>
</ul>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  it</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'shoud async delete api to todos a states'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">status</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> deleteTodo</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(status).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">200</span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"> /* HTML Response 200 OK */</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h2 id="상태관리-유닛테스트">상태관리 유닛테스트<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#상태관리-유닛테스트"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  it</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'should handle lists being added to a reducer empty state'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      lists: list.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">map</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">item</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({</span></span>

<li>
<p>Redux Action 유닛테스트</p>
<ul>
<li>redux action에 대한 유닛테스트 작성
<ul>
<li>리덕스 액션 수행 후 예상된 리턴값을 비교</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> slice, { initialState, fetchAction } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '..'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setupStore</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reducer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">preloadedState</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> configureStore</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    reducer: { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">reducer },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    preloadedState,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> list</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [];</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">describe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'lists redux state test'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setup</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      list,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      store: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">setupStore</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ lists: slice.reducer }, { initialState }), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// reducer 초기세팅</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  };</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  it</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'should handle lists being added fetchAction action state'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">store</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">list</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setup</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    store.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">dispatch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">fetchAction</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(list));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> nextState</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      ...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">initialState,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      lists: list.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">map</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">item</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">        ...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">item,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      })),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    };</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(store.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getState</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().lists).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toEqual</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(nextState);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      )[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> expectedActions</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        id,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      };</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">      expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(action.payload).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toEqual</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(expectedActions);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// fetch 사용</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> thunk </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'redux-thunk'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> fetchMock </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'fetch-mock'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { FETCH_TODOS } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '../constants'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> middlewares</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [thunk];</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">describe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'async actions test'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        userId: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        id: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        completed: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      },</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    fetchMock.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getOnce</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://jsonplaceholder.typicode.com/todos'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, {</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> store.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">dispatch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">fetchAsyncTodosAction</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">()).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // return of async actions</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // [0]: pending, [1]: FETCH_TODOS, [2]: fulfilled</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> responses</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> TodoAction</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">[] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> store.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getActions</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> list</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [];</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">describe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'&#x3C;List />'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    />);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  };</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    setupRender</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> list</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'list'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>

<p style="font-size: 25px; font-weight: bold;">react-query 유닛테스트</p>
<p>링크: <a href="https://github.com/TkDodo/testing-react-query/tree/main/src">testing-react-query/src at main · TkDodo/testing-react-query</a> (msw, jest)</p>
<ul>
<li>
<p>설치</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">npm</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> install</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> @testing-library/react-hooks</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> react-test-renderer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> --save-dev</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">or</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">yarn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> @testing-library/react-hooks</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> react-test-renderer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> --save-dev</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>For custom hooks</p>
<ul>
<li>@testing-library/react-hooks, @testing-library/react v13.1.0 이상일 경우 Custom Hook 테스트가 가능합니다. 해당 라이브러리를 사용하여서 wrapper로 hook를 감쌀 수 있으며 렌더링 시 테스트 구성요소를 감싸서 실행시켜 줍니다.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// @testing-library/react-hooks</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { renderHook, waitFor } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@testing-library/react-hooks'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// @testing-library/react v13.1.0 이상</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { renderHook, waitFor } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@testing-library/react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useCustomHook</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useQuery</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'customHook'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">], () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Hello'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> queryClient</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> QueryClient</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> wrapper</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">QueryClientProvider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> client</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">queryClient</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">QueryClientProvider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">result</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">waitFor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> renderHook</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useCustomHook</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(), { wrapper });</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> waitFor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> result.current.isSuccess);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(result.current.data).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toEqual</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Hello'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>Integration Test</p>
<ul>
<li>리액트 컴포넌트에서 실제 react-query api 테스팅 시 응답이 오기전에 테스트 팅이 수행되서 정상적인 테스트가 실행되지 않으므로 jest.mock을 사용해서 mock 데이터로 테스트를 진행한다.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { useTest } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> './hooks'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { testData } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> './mocks/testData'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> TestComponent </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> './TestComponent'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">jest.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">mock</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'./hooks'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> mockUseTest</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> useTest </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> jest</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">Mock</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">any</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// eslint-disable-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setup</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">QueryClientProvider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> client</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">queryClient</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">TestComponent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">QueryClientProvider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">beforeEach</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  mockUseTest.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">mockImplementation</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ isLoading: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">afterEach</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  jest.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">clearAllMocks</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">it</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'should TestComponent to element render'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  mockUseTest.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">mockImplementation</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    isLoading: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    data: testData.data,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }));</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  setup</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // When</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> testElement</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByTestId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'test'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // Then</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(testElement).not.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeUndefined</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // When</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> test1Element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByTestId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'test1'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // Then</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(test1Element).not.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeUndefined</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>Turn off retries</p>
<ul>
<li>리액트 쿼리는 기본적으로 3번의 재시도를 하도록 설정되어 있는데, 에러가 있는 쿼리에 경우에는 타임아웃이 걸리는 문제점을 있어서 초기 세팅에 retry값을 false로 설정합니다.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { QueryClient } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-query'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> queryClient</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> QueryClient</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  defaultOptions: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    queries: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // ✅ turns retries off</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      retry: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> wrapper</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">QueryClientProvider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> client</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">queryClient</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">QueryClientProvider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>turn-off-network-error-logging</p>
<ul>
<li>리액트 쿼리는 네트워크 에러에 대해서 콘솔 에러로 표기를 합니다. 해당 네트워크 에러와 테스트 에러가 같이 콘솔에 보일경우 혼란을 줄 수 있으므로, 테스트 환경세팅 시 로깅을 끄도록 설정합니다.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { QueryClient } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-query'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> queryClient</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> QueryClient</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  logger: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    log: console.log,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    warn: console.warn,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // ✅ no more errors on the console for tests</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    error: process.env.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">NODE_ENV</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'test'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {} </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.error,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>setQueryDefaults
useQuery에 옵션을 최대한 사용하지 하지 않고, 디폴드 옵션을 설정하도록 변경해야합니다. 단, 특정 쿼리에 대해서 옵션을 설정해야할 경우, queryClient.setQueryDefaults로 설정하세요</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> queryClient</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> QueryClient</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  defaultOptions: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    queries: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      retry: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ✅ only todos will retry 5 times</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">queryClient.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">setQueryDefaults</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'todos'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { retry: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> });</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> App</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">QueryClientProvider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> client</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">queryClient</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Example</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">QueryClientProvider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>Always await the query</p>
<ul>
<li>react-query는 비동기 처리를 하고 있기 때문에 응답값 중 isSuccess flag가 true 일경우 데이터에 결과를 얻을 수 있습니다.</li>
<li>해당 로직을 처리하기 위해서 @testing-library/react-hooks, @testing-library/react v13.1.0 이상의 라이브러리를 사용해서 테스트 코드 작성이 가능합니다.</li>
</ul>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">              })</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">              await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> waitFor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(result.current.isSuccess).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">))</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">              expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(result.current.data?.name).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'mocked-react-query'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">          })</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">          test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'failure query hook'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">              server.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">                  rest.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'*'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">req</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">res</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">ctx</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">                      return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> res</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(ctx.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">status</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">500</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">))</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">                  })</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">              )</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">              const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">result</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> renderHook</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useRepoData</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(), {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">                  wrapper: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">createWrapper</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">              })</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">              await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> waitFor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(result.current.isError).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">))</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">              expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(result.current.error).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeDefined</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">          })</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      })</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">      ```</span></span></code></pre></figure>

<h2 id="화면-단위-유닛테스트">화면 단위 유닛테스트<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#화면-단위-유닛테스트"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<p style="font-size: 25px; font-weight: bold;">패키지</p>
<ul>
<li>jest</li>
<li>react-test-renderer</li>
<li>@testing-library/react</li>
</ul>
<p style="font-size: 25px; font-weight: bold;">DOM Testing</p>
<ul>
<li>
<p>인터렉션(이벤트) 단위의 화면 테스트를 기준으로 한다.</p>

<li>
<p>Element Event Testing

<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="jsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="jsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> handleClick</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> jest.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">beforeEach</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>

<p>Element Event Testing</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Input Element Testing</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> React, { useState } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { render, fireEvent } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@testing-library/react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> CostInput</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">setValue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useState</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  removeDollarSign</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (value[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '$'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> value.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">slice</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> value);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  getReturnValue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (value </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> ''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> ''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> `$${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  handleChange</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> ev</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    ev.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">preventDefault</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> inputtedValue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ev.currentTarget.value;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> noDollarSign</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> removeDollarSign</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(inputtedValue);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">isNaN</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(noDollarSign)) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    setValue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getReturnValue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(noDollarSign));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  };</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">input</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> aria-label</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"cost-input"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> onChange</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">handleChange</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setup</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> utils</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">CostInput</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> input</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> utils.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByLabelText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'cost-input'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  fireEvent.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">change</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(input, { target: { value: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } });</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> idCounter </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> NumberDisplay</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useRef</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(idCounter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">++</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// to ensure we don't remount a different instance</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">span</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> data-testid</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"number-display"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">span</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">span</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> data-testid</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"instance-id"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">id.current</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">span</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'calling render with the same component on the same container does not remount'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">rerender</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">NumberDisplay</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByTestId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'number-display'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveTextContent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'1'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // re-render the same component with different props</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  rerender</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">NumberDisplay</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByTestId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'number-display'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveTextContent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'2'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByTestId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'instance-id'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveTextContent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'1'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
