<p style="font-size: 25px; font-weight: bold;">Snapshot Testing</p>
- package: react-test-renderer
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Link.js</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { useState } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> STATUS</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  HOVERED: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'hovered'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  NORMAL: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'normal'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> default</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Link</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">({ page, children }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">{</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">status</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">setStatus</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useState</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">STATUS</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">NORMAL</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>

<h2 id="react-유닛테스트">React 유닛테스트<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#react-유닛테스트"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<p style="font-size: 25px; font-weight: bold;">패키지</p>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { renderHook } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@testing-library/react-hooks'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Hello'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(result.current).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toEqual</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Hello'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p style="font-size: 25px; font-weight: bold;">ContextAPI Testing</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> React </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> NameContext</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> React.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">createContext</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Unknown'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> NameProvider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">first</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">last</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> fullName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> `${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">first</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">} ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">last</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">NameContext.Provider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">fullName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">NameContext.Provider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> NameConsumer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>My Name Is: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { NameContext, NameConsumer, NameProvider };</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">/**</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"> * Test default values by rendering a context consumer without a</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"> */</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'NameConsumer shows default value'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">NameConsumer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">^</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">My Name Is:/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveTextContent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'NameConsumer shows value from provider'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">NameContext.Provider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"C3P0"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">NameProvider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> first</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"Boba"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> last</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"Fett"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">NameContext.Consumer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">        {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">span</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Received: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">span</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">NameContext.Consumer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">NameProvider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">^</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">Received:/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).textContent).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">    'Received: Boba Fett'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">/**</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"> * A tree containing both a providers and consumer can be rendered normally</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"> */</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'NameProvider/Consumer shows name of character'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">NameProvider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> first</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"Leia"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> last</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"Organa"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">NameConsumer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">NameProvider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">^</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">My Name Is:/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).textContent).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">    'My Name Is: Leia Organa'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p style="font-size: 25px; font-weight: bold;">React-Router Testing</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {render </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> rtlRender, screen} </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@testing-library/react'</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '/some-route'</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">      render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">LocationDisplay</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />, {route})</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // avoid using test IDs when you can</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">      expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(screen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByTestId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'location-display'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveTextContent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(route)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    })</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">    ```</span></span></code></pre></figure>
